## paragraph:2

## paragraph:3

Bu belge POSEIDON hizmetinin kurum içi veya self-hosted ortamda kurulması, açıkça yetkilendirilmiş sürüm dağıtımı, yedek ve geri dönüş kanıtı, kontrollü trafik açılması, destek olayı ve tanılama erişiminin uygulanabilir veri ve işlem sözleşmesidir. Yazılım kabul kararı, dağıtım talebi, yürütücü alındısı, gözlenen teknik etki ve bağımsız kullanım doğrulaması ayrı kayıtlardır. Hiçbiri gemi veya römorkör operasyon izni vermez.

## paragraph:4

23.03 Deployment Environment, 23.04 Backup Restore DR ve 23.05 Production Monitoring Support runbookları kaynak olarak korunur. İlgili klasör 07_TEST_PILOT_RELEASE. Module37 system-health ve production-readiness sıfır giriş alanlı ekranları bu analizin kapalı query/read sözleşmesine bağlanır. Kaynaktaki birer action sayısı yetki veya mutation sayısı değildir. Bu analiz gerçek kurulum, çalışan anahtar deposu, yapılmış restore tatbikatı, müşteri SLA kabulü veya üretim güvenlik sertifikası değildir.

## paragraph:22

1 Ortak tipler ve kaynak sınırı

## paragraph:23

OPS-001. Yeni public uçlar COMMON-1 kullanır. UUID RFC4122; Version int64 1..9223372036854775807; Epoch int64≥0; Hash küçük harf SHA256 64 hex; Code ASCII [A-Z0-9_]{1,64}; ActionKey [a-z0-9_.]{1,120}; TextN Unicode NFC trim sonrası en çok N code point; Instant UTC RFC3339 milisaniye; DurationSeconds int64 0..315360000; IANA timezone kayıtlı zone adı. Boolean stringe çevrilmez. Bilinmeyen property, duplicate JSON key ve NaN/Infinity 400; enum/bound/çapraz alan hatası 422. ? yok olabilir; null yalnız açıkça nullable alanda. Hash, ref veya UI rol etiketi yetki değildir.

## paragraph:24

RevisionRef={owner:OwnerCode,kind:Code,id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,version:Version,contentHash:Hash}. OwnerCode=17.01|52.01|52.02|53.01|54.01|54.02|54.03|55.01|58.01|58.02|58.03|59.01|59.04|60.01|61.01|63.02|63.03|63.06|65.01|65.02. Her kullanım owner+kind için aşağıda kapalı allowlist taşır; enumda olmak her ref slotunda kabul edilmek değildir. EvidenceRef RevisionRef owner58.02 kind EVIDENCE_REVISION; RunbookRef owner58.02 kind DOCUMENT_REVISION; EnvironmentRef owner65.01 kind ENVIRONMENT_REVISION. ForeignRef yalnız source read+purpose erişimi doğrulandığında görünür.

## paragraph:25

Scope={companyId:UUID,vesselIds:UUID[0..1000]}; sıralı benzersiz vesselIds boşsa company hizmeti, tüm gemiler wildcardı değildir. tenantId oturumdan; body actor/tenant/user rolü seçmez. TargetScope={environmentRef:EnvironmentRef,scope:Scope}; ortam sahipliği şirketiyle eşit olmalıdır. SourceFence={tenantId:serverUUID,principalId:serverUUID,personId:serverUUID,authorizationEpoch:Epoch,environmentRef,deploymentGeneration:UUID,environmentEpoch:Epoch,sourceVector:[{ref:RevisionRef,epoch:Epoch,validUntil:Instant|null}][1..1000],sourceVectorHash:Hash,issuedAt:Instant,validUntil:Instant}. Server canonical sıralı vector üretir; client supplied epoch assertion yetki sağlamaz.

## paragraph:26

Current olumlu commit; original human current grant ve scope/fields, exact job/version, ortam generation/epoch, source revision/hash/validity ve required-set selectorı tek authoritative transaction veya eşdeğer synchronous compare-and-commit fence içinde yeniden doğrular. GET sonra write, JWT role, TTL veya eventual event aynı fence değildir. Fence kurulamazsa 503 FENCE_UNAVAILABLE, callback ve outbox yazımı rollback. Opaque proof TTL en çok30 saniye olup min source validityyi aşmaz. Revocation committen önce kazanırsa olumlu effect dispatch edilmez. Committen sonra revocation dispatch iznini hemen keser, başlamış effect geriye olmuş sayılmaz; reconcile gerekir.

## paragraph:27

FactClock={precision:EXACT,at:Instant,source:TRUSTED_COLLECTOR|SIGNED_EXECUTOR,clockEvidenceRef:EvidenceRef,uncertaintyMs:int0..60000} veya {precision:REPORTED,at:Instant,source:HUMAN,timezone:IANA,reason:Text200} veya {precision:DATE_ONLY,date:YYYY-MM-DD,timezone:IANA} veya {precision:UNKNOWN,reasonCode:Code}. Hiçbir UNKNOWN/DATE_ONLY saat uydurulmaz. İmzalı yürütücü saatinin kalibrasyonu doğrulanmamışsa EXACT kabul edilmez; precision=UNKNOWN, reasonCode=CLOCK_EVIDENCE_UNVERIFIED kullanılır. Ham bildirilen saat ve imza kendi kaynak kanıtında korunur. REPORTED yalnız gerçekten insan tarafından beyan edilmiş olgunun HUMAN kaynağı içindir; yürütücü gözlemi insan beyanına dönüştürülmez. recordedAt daima server Instant, olayın gerçek zamanı yerine geçmez.

## paragraph:28

SafeLink={routeKey:ENVIRONMENT|OPS_CHECK|DEPLOYMENT|RECOVERY|SUPPORT_CASE|DIAGNOSTIC|CYBER_CASE|INTEGRATION|MOBILE_RECOVERY|RELEASE_CANDIDATE|CONTROLLED_DOCUMENT|ACCESS_REQUEST,id:UUID,revisionId:UUID|null}; owner kayıtlı UI routerı üretir. External URL, hostname, IP, filesystem path, query secret, shell veya SQL metni bu shape içinde yoktur. Links yalnız current source read mevcutken üretilir. Kaynak unavailable ise boş güncel liste/READY üretmek yerine PARTIAL veya UNAVAILABLE ve görünür güvenli reasonCode döner.

## paragraph:29

2 Ortam revizyonu ve işletim hedefleri

## paragraph:30

OPS-002. Environment root={id,companyId,code:Code,createdAt,version,currentRevisionRef:EnvironmentRef|null,epoch:Epoch,state:ACTIVE|SUSPENDED|RETIRED}. Root code/company değişmez. Revision={id,revisionNo,rootId,baseRevisionRef:EnvironmentRef|null,body:EnvironmentMaterial,bodyHash,state:DRAFT|IN_REVIEW|APPROVED|EFFECTIVE|REJECTED|WITHDRAWN,approvalSetRef:RevisionRef|null,createdByPersonId,submittedAt:null|Instant,originallyDeclaredEffectiveFrom:Instant|null,originallyDeclaredEffectiveTo:Instant|null}. EFFECTIVE body/hash değişmez. Current selection ayrı append-only ActivationEvent={id,newRevisionRef,previousRevisionRef:null|EnvironmentRef,effectiveAt,recordedAt,actorPersonId}; selectedUntil sonraki cutoverdan türetilir. Gelecek aktivasyon v1 yoktur; activate yalnız server now ile atomik CAS. Eski revizyonun imzası ve body alanları değiştirilmez.

## paragraph:31

EnvironmentMaterial={name:Text3..120,stage:DEV|TEST|UAT|PILOT|PROD,hosting:ON_PREM|SELF_HOSTED|DEV_TEST_CLOUD,scope:Scope,deploymentEndpointRef:RevisionRef,componentManifest:ComponentEntry[1..100],config:ConfigBinding,supportMatrixRef:RunbookRef,runbookRefs:RunbookRef[1..20],metricThresholds:MetricThreshold[1..1000],backupSchedules:BackupSchedule[1..100],objectives:ObjectiveEntry[1..100],responseObjectives:ResponseObjective[4],requiredCheckPolicy:CheckRequirement[1..100],executionAdapterRef:RevisionRef,recoveryAdapterRef:RevisionRef,approvalMandatePolicyRef:RevisionRef}. deploymentEndpointRef/adapter refs owner65.01 kind REGISTERED_EXECUTOR, publisher deployment registration; opaque identifier only, credential/network details secret storede. Keyfi endpoint veya komut body yoktur. DEV_TEST_CLOUD yalnız DEV/TEST; PILOT/PROD gerçek veri scope ve deployment authority kabulü ister. Support matrix gerçek kabul edilmiş browser/server build aralıklarını RunbookRef ile pinler; doküman varlığı test PASS değildir.

## paragraph:32

ComponentEntry={componentId:UUID,code:Code,kind:API|DB|EVIDENCE_STORE|AUTH|AUDIT_OUTBOX|INTEGRATION|MOBILE|ANALYTICS|SCHEDULER|INFRASTRUCTURE,tier:T0|T1|T2,sourceOwner:OwnerCode,critical:boolean,backupDisposition:REQUIRED|REBUILDABLE,dependencyComponentIds:UUID[0..100]}. Tüm dependencyler aynı manifestte, duplicate/cycle422. ANALYTICS component sourceOwner=55.01;55.01 yalnız ANALYTICS source/check/read slotsunda geçerlidir, diğer participant/authority ref slotlarına wildcard eklemez. REBUILDABLE yalnız ANALYTICS gibi approved rebuild planı ve authoritative inputs kanıtıyla; audit/receipt/revocation/source identity ledgerları rebuildable ilan edilemez. Manifest full required populationdır; UI visible subset manifest oluşturamaz.

## paragraph:33

ConfigBinding={configHash:Hash,schemaHash:Hash,configManifestRef:RunbookRef,migrationPlanRef:RunbookRef,rollbackPlanRef:RunbookRef,secretInventoryRef:RevisionRef}. SecretInventoryRef owner65.01 kind SECRET_INVENTORY_DESCRIPTOR içerik hashı ve revision metadata taşır; secret value, username, token, full key path yoktur. Descriptor issuer kayıtlı secret-store adapterı; handle başına availability, expiry ve recovery-test receipt current bilgisi yetkili checkte okunur, generic dashboarda taşınmaz. Secret creation/rotation/recovery kendiliğinden yapılmaz; exact authorized operational change ve mevcut secret-store/IAM süreci gerekir. Missing adapter NOT_CONFIGURED, olumlu readiness yok.

## paragraph:34

MetricThreshold={componentId:UUID,signalKind:HealthSignal.kind,aggregation:LAST|AVG|P50|P95|SUM|RATE,unit:MILLISECONDS|SECONDS|PERCENT|COUNT,windowSeconds:int1..86400,comparison:LT|LE|GT|GE|EQ,limit:DecimalString,minimumSamples:int1..1000000,maximumAgeSeconds:int1..86400,procedureRef:RunbookRef}. Unique component+signalKind+aggregation; rate/percent denominator and source sample eligibility exact registered procedure schema specifies, unresolved schema UNKNOWN. Numeric bounds follow MetricValue; no implicit threshold default. BackupSchedule={componentId,mode:ENCRYPTED_BASE_WAL|VERSIONED_OBJECTS|SIGNED_CONFIG_EXPORT|LEDGER_SNAPSHOT|KEY_PROVIDER_RECOVERY,intervalSeconds:int60..31536000,maximumLagSeconds:int1..31536000,providerRegistrationRef:RevisionRef,retentionPolicyRef:RunbookRef,consistencyGroup:Code}; component-specific mode, full manifest and secret recovery opaque descriptor; schedule receipt not backup success. Current next-due server-derived, missed capture alert cannot fabricate point.

## paragraph:35

ObjectiveEntry={componentId:UUID,rpo:ObjectiveValue,rto:ObjectiveValue,restoreTestCadenceSeconds:int86400..31536000|null,objectiveBasis:DESIGN_PROVISIONAL|APPROVED_OPERATIONAL,contractRef:RunbookRef|null}. ObjectiveValue={kind:FINITE,seconds:DurationSeconds} veya {kind:REBUILDABLE,planRef:RunbookRef} veya {kind:UNSET}. REBUILDABLE sadece rpo ve component backupDisposition REBUILDABLE için; rto FINITE veya UNSET. Sıfır ancak açıkça onaylanmış sıfır kayıp/süre hedefidir, UNKNOWN değildir. APPROVED_OPERATIONAL sadece bağımsız ortam approval tamamlandıktan sonra server read projectionda kullanılır; client body objectiveBasis=DESIGN_PROVISIONAL, submit sonrası receipt approved interpretation sağlar. contractRef müşteri SLA onayı sayılmaz; bu modül sözleşme imzalamaz.

## paragraph:36

Kaynak başlangıç önerileri T0 RPO900s/RTO14400s, T1 RPO3600s/RTO28800s, T2 rebuildable/RTO86400s olarak yeni draftta isteğe bağlı provisional öneri gösterilir; otomatik etkin değer değildir. Approved hedef yoksa objective comparison UNKNOWN ve production authorize bloklu. Test ölçümü bu hedef alanlarına yazılamaz; farklı objective revizyonuyla geçmiş test sonradan PASSa dönüştürülmez. Yeni değerlendirme exact eski ölçüm + yeni objectiveRef ile yeni receipt üretir ve geçmiş verdicti korur.

## paragraph:37

ResponseObjective={severity:P0|P1|P2|P3,targetSeconds:int1..31536000,clock:ELAPSED|BUSINESS,calendar:null|BusinessCalendar}. Dört severity birer kez zorunlu. BusinessCalendar={timezone:IANA,coverageFrom:Instant,coverageUntil:Instant,workingIntervals:[{from:Instant,to:Instant}][1..10000],hash:Hash}; approved scope için UTC, çakışmayan sıralı aralıklar. ELAPSED calendar=null. BUSINESS deadline server interval toplamıyla; olay coverage dışındaysa UNKNOWN, yeni yıl/holiday tahmini yok. Kaynak15dk/1saat/4işsaati/1işgünü önerileri burada kullanıcı draft önerisidir; “iş günü” exact onaylı çalışma aralığı/saniyesi olmadan sayıya çevrilmez. SLA breach etiketi yerine operational response target elapsed/unknown gösterilir.

## paragraph:38

CheckRequirement={slotCode:CheckKind,componentId:UUID|null,purpose:INSTALL_ACCEPTANCE|PRE_DEPLOY|POST_DEPLOY|RECOVERY_ISOLATED|RECOVERY_RESUME,required:boolean,validForSeconds:int1..31536000,sourceContractRef:RevisionRef,applicability:REQUIRED|NOT_APPLICABLE,notApplicableReason:Text10..1000|null,mandateSlot:ENGINEERING|PRODUCT|SECURITY|OPERATIONS}. Required false yalnız informational checkte; hard floor aşağıda daraltılamaz. NOT_APPLICABLE receiptı exact component/purpose için bağımsız environment approval altında ve hard floor dışındaki slotta mümkündür. Policy ambiguity UNKNOWN, boş set PASS değil.

## paragraph:39

Approval slotları ENGINEERING ve PRODUCT bütün UAT/PILOT/PROD environment revisionlarda, SECURITY auth/keys/tenancy veya scope expansion değişiminde ayrıca zorunludur. OPERATIONS restore/support planı için zorunludur. Person aynı revision için en çokbir slot doldurur, maker≠all approvers. Current54.02 ops.environment.approve.<slot> AND ops.environment.approve qualification,52.01 current mandate assignment exact scope; platform admin etiketi yeterli değildir. IN_REVIEW→APPROVED server full required vote set tamamlayınca; herhangi REJECT→REJECTED, RETURN→DRAFT ve eski vote set VOID. Draft edit base version CAS, submitted revision immutable; returned yeni draft revision olur. APPROVED→EFFECTIVE current independent activation authority ve same source fence; suspend current kullanımını hemen keser, eski approval tarihçesi silinmez.

## paragraph:40

3 Yapılandırma seçimi ve kontrollü değişiklik

## paragraph:41

OPS-003. ApprovedEnvironmentSet={id:deterministicUUID,revisionId:deterministicUUID,contentHash:Hash,targetEnvironmentRef:EnvironmentRef,entries:[{environmentRef:EnvironmentRef,configHash:Hash,schemaHash:Hash,approvalRef:RevisionRef}][1..100]}. Entryler environment root id ASC, her root tek current effective revision; target listede tam bir kez. Canonical body target+ordered entries; id/revision UUIDv5 namespace OPS_CONFIG_SET_V1 + contentHash; no editable master. ConfigProfileRead request {targetEnvironmentRef,environmentRefs:EnvironmentRef[1..100],expectedConfigProfileHash:Hash|null,actorContext:TrustedActorContext}; result CURRENT {profile:ApprovedEnvironmentSet,checkedAt,validUntil,sourceFence} veya BLOCKED {reasonCode,checkedAt}. 65.02 manifest configProfileHash=bu canonical contentHash; ayrı environmentda test edilen artifact aynı artifactSetHashla ve approved config/schema bindingiyle doğrulanır. “Latest config” fallback yoktur.

## paragraph:42

Environment revizyonu config/scope/secret inventory veya component değişince epoch artırır, current readiness ve non-dispatched authorizations invalid olur. Runtime root observedConfigHash ayrı measured effecttir; revision activate prod configi değiştirmez. Config apply/migration yalnız exact deployment job planından yürür. Önceki immutable artifact tekrar build edilmez. Signed artifact descriptor digest, dependency manifest, schema plan ve config binding65.02 manifestiyle exact eşleşir; version label eşitliği hash eşitliği yerine geçmez.

## paragraph:43

Production manual DDL veya doğrudan veri düzeltmesi sıradan destek eylemi değildir. V1 otomatik arbitrary SQL/shell executor yoktur. EmergencyChangePlan={incidentRef:RevisionRef,controlledProcedureRef:RunbookRef,changeDigest:Hash,targetEnvironmentRef,affectedComponentIds:UUID[1..100],backupPointRef:RevisionRef,expectedBeforeHash:Hash,expectedAfterHash:Hash,rollbackStrategy:REVERSIBLE|RESTORE|FORWARD_FIX,independentApprovalRefs:RevisionRef[2..4]}. Emergency change deployment job kind EMERGENCY_CHANGE altında kaydedilir; yazılım artifact değişiyorsa65.02 current GO zorunlu. Değişmiyorsa accepted current build baseline ve iki bağımsız OPERATIONS/SECURITY authorization şarttır. Bu istisna tenant isolation, source owner write/audit/hold veya operational safety sınırını gevşetmez. Gerçek system security setting veya secret kullanım yetkisi ayrıca kendi sahibindedir.

## paragraph:44

4 Yedek noktası ve tam kurtarma kümesi

## paragraph:45

OPS-004. BackupPoint={id,revisionId,environmentRef,deploymentGeneration,backupSetHash:Hash,createdAt,consistentAt:FactClock,state:RECORDED|VERIFIED|INVALID|INCONCLUSIVE,components:BackupComponent[1..100],sourceEpoch:Epoch,verificationRef:RevisionRef|null}. BackupComponent={componentId,kind:DB_BASE_WAL|EVIDENCE_OBJECTS|CONFIG_REFERENCE|AUDIT_OUTBOX|INTEGRATION_LEDGER|MOBILE_CUSTODY|APPROVED_IMPORTS|SECRET_RECOVERY_HANDLE,manifestRef:RevisionRef,manifestHash:Hash,captureBoundary:FactClock,consistencyGroup:Code,encrypted:boolean,keyRecoveryDescriptorRef:RevisionRef|null,integrity:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN}. ManifestRef owner65.01 provider descriptor,58.02evidence custody,58.01source custody,63.02ledger veya63.03checkpoint türüyle exact slot allowlist. Secrets için sadece opaque recovery descriptor, plaintext export yok. Encrypted=false veya unresolved key inventory olumlu verificationı bloklar.

## paragraph:46

Backup import payloadı clientın başarı beyanı değil trusted adapter descriptorına referanstır: BackupRegisterDto={environmentRef,providerBackupRef:RevisionRef,expectedBackupSetHash:Hash,scope,recordedReason:Text10..1000}. Provider adapter registered deployment schemahashıyla complete component manifest üretir; current source custody/hold/readpurpose doğrulanır. Human eski backup intake sadece EvidenceRef ve reported boundaries ile INCONCLUSIVE kayıt açar, production recovery point olmaz. Verify bağımsız checker descriptor signature/hash, full component coverage, matching recovery boundaries, decryptability proof ve critical ledger completenessi kontrol eder. Yedek checksum PASS restore edilebilirlik PASS değildir.

## paragraph:47

CompleteBackupSet full-set çözümü environment component manifestinden server yapılır. DB/WAL, evidence binaries+metadata+hold state, config, auth revocation, audit/outbox effect ledger, source identity/dedup tombstones ve mobile custody/keys gerekli ise aynı consistency group/boundaryde bulunur. Eksik/grantı okunamayan bileşen hidden countla sızdırılmaz; aggregate UNKNOWN, positive blocked. Restore sırasında58.02 hold/disposition ve source retention current authoritative durumuyla uzlaştırılır; eski backup silinmiş içeriği yeniden erişilebilir yapamaz. Fiziki backup retention yetkili policyye göre58.02/provider sahibinde kalır,65.01 hardcoded yıl/süre koymaz.

## paragraph:48

BackupPlan cadence actual scheduling platformadapterında declared source manifesttir. Başarılı scheduling receipt backup existence değildir; missed run health signal UNKNOWN/FAIL source conditionına göre. Backup point supersession geçmişi silmez. Yeni applicable failed/unknown backup veya restore testi, eski PASSı seçip gizlenemez: current latest applicable evaluation set, known FAIL precedence; unknown fresh result varsa previous PASS current kullanılmaz. Eski receipt yalnız historical etiketli ve current disclosure izniyle okunur.

## paragraph:49

5 İşletim testi ölçüm ve bağımsız doğrulama

## paragraph:50

OPS-005. CheckKind=TLS_TRUST|AUTH_TENANT|KEY_RECOVERY|BACKUP_INTEGRITY|RESTORE_COMPLETENESS|MONITORING_COVERAGE|INTEGRATION_RECONCILIATION|MIGRATION_REVERSIBILITY|BUSINESS_SMOKE|NOTICE_READY|ONCALL_ROLLBACK|MOBILE_RECONCILIATION|DATA_PROVENANCE|ENVIRONMENT_HEALTH. OperationalCheck={id,revisionId,version,kind,environmentRef,deploymentGeneration,purpose,componentIds:UUID[0..100],context:CheckContext,supersedesCheckRef:RevisionRef|null,requiredItemSetRef:RevisionRef,requiredItemSetHash:Hash,observations:CheckObservation[1..1000],measurement:RecoveryMeasurement|null,evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[1..50],state:DRAFT|SUBMITTED|VALID|INVALID|INCONCLUSIVE|WITHDRAWN,verificationRef:null|RevisionRef,createdByPersonId,recordedAt}. CheckContext={kind:ENVIRONMENT} veya {kind:BACKUP,backupPointRef:RevisionRef} veya {kind:DEPLOYMENT,jobRef:RevisionRef} veya {kind:RECOVERY,jobRef:RevisionRef,backupPointRef:RevisionRef}. Request exact context sağlar, full required set client seçmez.

## paragraph:51

RequiredCheckItemSet producer environment approved check policy + registered test-procedure schema resolverıdır. Immutable descriptor={id,revisionId,contextHash,policyRef,procedureRef:RunbookRef,items:[{itemId:UUID,code:Code,componentId:UUID|null,mandatory:boolean}][1..1000],setHash,complete:boolean}. Mandatory hard floor her tür için closed deployment manifestte: TLS chain/hostname/expiry; AUTH tenant isolation/revocation/role negative; KEY recovery availability and no plaintext; BACKUP full consistency; RESTORE schema+evidence+hold+audit+dedup; MONITORING signal collection+alert delivery; INTEGRATION outbox identities+unknown effects; MIGRATION dry-run+reversibility strategy; BUSINESS_SMOKE exact registered domain fixture; NOTICE approved audience/content+delivery readiness; ONCALL current roster+rollback procedure; MOBILE exact63.03checkpoint; PROVENANCE owner identity/hash; HEALTH configured required probes. Resolver absent/partial UNKNOWN, empty items rejected; new test type deployment/schema change requires reviewed registry, not user free enum.

## paragraph:52

CheckObservation={itemId:UUID,result:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN|NOT_APPLICABLE,observedAt:FactClock,evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[0..10],reason:Text0..2000}. Exactly each required item once; duplicate422, absent produces UNKNOWN row, unknown item422. NOT_APPLICABLE only item applicability from approved required set, not actor choice. Ingested client PASS remains asserted observation until independent verify. Known required FAIL yields overall FAIL even with unknowns; else required unknown/stale/hidden/missing yields UNKNOWN; else all required PASS yields PASS; all N/A never PASS. Independent verifier exact observed/source manifest cannot approve favorable subset. SoD actual evidence collector/executor/maker≠verifier.

## paragraph:53

RecoveryMeasurement={kind:MEASURED,measurementContext:EXERCISE|ACTUAL,lossReferenceAt:FactClock,restoredDurableThrough:FactClock,serviceUnavailableAt:FactClock,usableServiceVerifiedAt:FactClock,observedLossSeconds:DurationSeconds,observedRecoverySeconds:DurationSeconds,objectiveRef:EnvironmentRef,calculationHash:Hash} veya {kind:UNMEASURABLE,reason:SOURCE_CLOCK_UNKNOWN|BOUNDARY_UNPROVEN|PARTIAL_DATA|NOT_A_RECOVERY_TEST,availableClockRefs:EvidenceRef[0..10]}. MEASURED requires all clocks EXACT and trusted corresponding event semantics, ordered durableThrough≤lossReference and unavailable≤usableVerified; negative values422, no clamp. RPO measured loss = lossReferenceAt − restoredDurableThrough; RTO = usableServiceVerifiedAt − serviceUnavailableAt, including detection/authorization/restore/validation delay, not just copy duration. Clock uncertainty produces upperBoundSeconds=ceil(delta + both uncertaintyMs/1000), lowerBound=max(0,floor(delta−both)); observed fields use upper bound for conservative objective comparison. Raw clock refs/hash preserve original values. Boundary proof must include all authoritative components; fastest component does not define service recovery.

## paragraph:54

Exercise with no actual outage uses declared simulated event timestamps and context purpose RECOVERY_ISOLATED; measurement labels EXERCISE, cannot appear actual production RTO. Persisted measurementContext server tarafından exact job contextiyle doğrulanır. Partial component data loss UNKNOWN; source last transaction timestamp vs wall-clock alone is not durable boundary proof. Objective comparison={result:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN,objectiveRef,measuredUpperSeconds:null|int,limitSeconds:null|int,basisHash}; only approved FINITE exact objective and trustworthy MEASURED yields PASS/FAIL, otherwiseUNKNOWN. Target, observed result, verification result and currentUse are separate fields.

## paragraph:55

VerificationReceipt={id,revisionId,subjectRef:RevisionRef,subjectHash:Hash,requiredSetRef,coverageHash,decision:VALID|INVALID|INCONCLUSIVE,decisionActorPersonId:UUID,mandateAssignmentRef:RevisionRef,reason:Text10..2000,verifiedAt,validUntil,sourceVectorHash,verificationEpoch:Epoch}. VALID requires complete current scope/evidence and required aggregate PASS; INVALID captures known failure, INCONCLUSIVE missing/unproven. VALID does not permanently certify source after expiry/change. Correction creates new OperationalCheck revision; no overwrite of signed observations. Withdraw immediate current-use BLOCKED; adverse later check invalidates applicable prior selection at its effective observation boundary, not retroactive rewrite.

6 Tam küme işletim hazırlığı

## paragraph:57

OPS-006. AssessmentRequest={environmentRef,purpose:INSTALL_ACCEPTANCE|PRE_DEPLOY|POST_DEPLOY|RECOVERY_ISOLATED|RECOVERY_RESUME,context:CheckContext}. Server resolves required slots from approved environment policy and mandatory floor, selects latest applicable immutable verified check per slot/component/context, then persists OperationalAssessment={id,revisionId,environmentRef,deploymentGeneration,context,purpose,policyRef,requiredSetRef,requiredSetHash,slots:AssessmentSlot[1..1000],coverage:COMPLETE|INCOMPLETE|UNKNOWN,overall:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN,evaluatedAt,validUntil,sourceVectorHash,sourceEpoch}. Slot={slotId:UUID,kind,componentId:null|UUID,required:boolean,applicability:REQUIRED|NOT_APPLICABLE,selectedCheckRef:null|RevisionRef,verificationRef:null|RevisionRef,result:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN|NOT_APPLICABLE,reasonCode:Code}. Hidden slot/evidence is not disclosed individually without source rights; whole assessment positive port requires full source coverage and access.

## paragraph:58

PRE_DEPLOY operational hard floor: approved env/config, current verified recovery point, migration reversibility, monitoring+oncall, current auth/tenant and key recovery, source integration readiness, approved notice readiness when impact requires, rollback plan. POST_DEPLOY additionally actual hash/schema/config observation and business smoke. RECOVERY_ISOLATED requires full backup restore integrity, data provenance, hold/auth revocation, audit/outbox/dedup, integration and mobile checkpoints where present. RECOVERY_RESUME değerlendirmesi, izolasyon kontrollerine ek olarak gerçek hedefin güncel sağlığını, güncel erişim iptali kaynağını ve tam gerekli katılımcı kümesinin VERIFIED checkpointleriyle RecoveryReconciliation kanıtını denetler. Bu, yeniden başlatma yetkilendirmesinden önceki teknik değerlendirmedir. Aynı recovery job için ReleaseAuthorizationRecord, PREPARED veya RESUMED makbuzu bu değerlendirmeye girdi ya da PASS önkoşulu olamaz; aksi durumda değerlendirme ile yetkilendirme arasında döngü oluşur. Bağımsız insanın yeni RECOVERY_RESUME yetkilendirmesi §11 uyarınca doğrulamadan sonra ayrıca üretilir. Her katılımcının prepare/resume kaydı ve gerçek trafik açılması sonraki ayrı yetki, kaynak ve makbuz kapılarına bağlı kalır; assessment PASS bunların yerine geçmez. No broad technical admin exception. 65.02 final acceptance decision/proof is not a member of OperationalAssessment source graph. New-release deployment authorization separately ANDs current65.02GO proof with this operational assessment;65.02 may consume the independent operational proof without a circular GO→ops→GO dependency. Build identity is a manifest fact, not a final approval slot. Manual APPROVE on assessment does not exist; assessment derived from independently verified source checks.

## paragraph:59

Selector rule latest applicable check recordedAt DESC, revisionId DESC within exact environment generation/context/kind/component and approved procedure. An INVALID check after old VALID remains FAIL until new independently verified corrective check explicitly supersedes that failing check through same full-set context. New INCONCLUSIVE blocks old PASS even if old validUntil future. Future observation not yet effective excluded, historical source never current substituted. Evaluation coverage determined before display filtering. Known visible/authorized required FAIL yields FAIL; otherwise any unreadable source prevents positive aggregate and yields UNKNOWN without hidden reasons/count. A source FAIL remains conservative blocker internally even when viewer cannot see details.

## paragraph:60

Assessment is immutable snapshot, current use derived separately={state:VALID|BLOCKED|EXPIRED|UNKNOWN,checkedAt,validUntil,reasonCode}. Environment epoch, auth revocation, source check correction/withdrawal/new applicable failure, expired test cadence or required-set change fences current-use. Expiry worker delay never prolongs validity: read/commit computes now against boundaries. GateEvidencePort below returns current owner-reported assessment and never recomputes a different favorable subset in65.02.

OPS-007. Plan={candidateBinding:CandidateBinding,artifactSetHash,dependencyManifestHash,configProfileHash,schemaPlanHash,preChangeBackupRef:RevisionRef,preDeployAssessmentRef:RevisionRef,window:{from:Instant,to:Instant},steps:ExecutionStep[1..100],rollbackStrategy:REVERSIBLE|RESTORE|FORWARD_FIX,rollbackRunbookRef:RunbookRef,noticeReadinessCheckRef:RevisionRef|null,emergencyChange:EmergencyChangePlan|null}. Window positive≤24h; candidate binding below exact; plan edit only DRAFT, submit freezes body/hash; source change new draft revision and review, not receipt patch.

## paragraph:63

CandidateBinding={kind:NEW_RELEASE,candidateRef:RevisionRef owner65.02,decisionRef:RevisionRef owner65.02,manifestHash:Hash} veya {kind:UNCHANGED_BUILD,baselinePromotionReceiptRef:RevisionRef owner65.01,expectedArtifactSetHash:Hash}. PROMOTION always NEW_RELEASE. EMERGENCY_CHANGE permits UNCHANGED_BUILD only config/data maintenance with unchanged artifacts and current accepted build baseline; platform feature/code/schema incompatibility cannot be relabeled unchanged. Schema alteration still approved exact plan and65.02 change-assessment source, requiring NEW_RELEASE when accepted manifest no longer matches. Unknown baseline blocks dispatch.

## paragraph:64

ExecutionStep strict union: NewExecutionStep={mode:NEW,stepId:UUID,ordinal:int1..100,kind:StepKind,executorOperationRef:RevisionRef,expectedInputHash:Hash,expectedOutputHash:Hash|null,componentIds:UUID[1..100],rollbackStepRef:UUID|null}; ReusedExecutionStep={mode:REUSED,stepId:UUID,ordinal:int1..100,kind:StepKind,reusedStepReceiptRef:RevisionRef,originalEffectId:UUID,originalSemanticStepHash:Hash,expectedInputHash:Hash,expectedOutputHash:Hash,componentIds:UUID[1..100]}. StepKind=DRAIN_TRAFFIC|APPLY_ARTIFACT|APPLY_CONFIG|APPLY_MIGRATION|RUN_REGISTERED_SMOKE|RESTORE_ISOLATED|RESTORE_TARGET|RECONCILE|OPEN_TRAFFIC|CLOSE_TRAFFIC. REUSED has no executableOperation/rollback dispatch payload. Source receipt must be current disclosed CONFIRMED/COMPLETE exact environment+generation+component+semantic hash; persisted effect remains materialized and has not been reversed by later step. Validator rejects mismatching source/hash/generation, duplicate originalEffectId and NEW entry matching already successful semantic identity in lineage. Scheduler marks REUSED satisfied by source receipt without generating dispatch. Reused refs top-level list equals ordered REUSED entries; no loose advisory list. Step ref owner65.01 kind REGISTERED_OPERATION, approved deployment manifest maps exact typed operation; no arbitrary arguments/string script. OPEN_TRAFFIC not executable in initial deployment plan; separate traffic release command derives its own single step only after VERIFIED. New environment promotion ordering drain→artifact/config/migration→smoke is validated against registered plan schema. Reversible dependency graph order cannot skip mandatory stage; forward fix/restore need separate authorized job, never “undo all” button.

## paragraph:65

DeploymentAuthorizeDto={expectedPlanHash:Hash,acceptanceProof:Text200|null,preDeployAssessmentRef:RevisionRef,validUntil:Instant,reason:Text10..2000}. For NEW_RELEASE65.02 RELEASE_ACCEPTANCE_PROOF_V1.WithAcceptanceFence exact current GO and all proof hashes plus65.01current PRE_DEPLOY assessment/environment/plan/source fence hold through commit. UNCHANGED_BUILD acceptanceProof=null and current baseline+independent OPERATIONS/SECURITY approved emergency intent required. Authorizer not maker/executor; exact ops.deployment.authorize AND ops.release_authority mandate, environment stage/scope. No client decision actor. AuthorizationRecord={id,revisionId,purpose:DEPLOY_EXECUTION,jobRef,planHash,environmentRef,expectedDeploymentGeneration,candidateBinding,assessmentRef,sourceVectorHash,authorizedByPersonId,mandateRef,issuedAt,validUntil,epoch,state:ACTIVE|REVOKED|EXPIRED|CONSUMED}. This is operational execution authorization, not traffic or vessel release.

## paragraph:66

NEW_RELEASE admission uses65.02 RELEASE_ACCEPTANCE_PROOF_V1.ReadCurrent request {candidateId,decisionRevisionId,targetEnvironmentRef,purpose:DEPLOYMENT_PROMOTION,consumerActorContext:TrustedActorContext}; strict current GO proof binds candidate/decision/assessment/manifest/artifact/dependency/config/schema/source hashes, target, original person, auth/acceptance epochs and validity. WithAcceptanceFence request {proof:Text200,expectedCandidateRef,expectedDecisionRef,expectedManifestHash:Hash,expectedAcceptanceEpoch:Epoch,consumerActorContext}; consumerCommitContext={consumerOwner:65.01,operation:DEPLOYMENT_AUTHORIZE|DISPATCH_ADMIT|STEP_ADMIT|TRAFFIC_AUTHORIZE,deploymentJobId:UUID,expectedJobVersion:Version,commandId:UUID,payloadHash:Hash,targetEnvironmentRef:EnvironmentRef}. Source65.02wire refs are converted by OPS_RELEASE_ADAPTER_V1 exactly, not local redefinition of producer fields.

## paragraph:67

All four admissions use a fresh current acceptance proof and recursively58.02 RELEASE_POLICY_PORT_V1 policy/source/approval/auth fences, plus65.01operational source/current authority fence, held through one authoritative admission commit. 65.02 supplies58.02 child context consumerOwner65.02,candidateId/currentCandidateVersion,same commandId/payloadHash and operation ACCEPTANCE_PROOF_COMMIT; server-only parentCommitContext={owner:65.01,operation:the same four-value enum,deploymentJobId,expectedJobVersion,commandId,payloadHash,targetEnvironmentRef:source PolicyRef}. Child cannot change parent target/intent. All required policy proofs acquired together in deterministic order; no independent sequential transactions counted as atomic. Fresh proof unavailable/source revoked means rollback and503/409, even if original GO or deployment authorization remains historically recorded.

## paragraph:68

DEPLOYMENT_AUTHORIZE binds authorizing human; DISPATCH_ADMIT binds current dispatch initiating human, who may differ but must have current exact grant; STEP_ADMIT service retains dispatch initiating human context and65.01also checks original authorizer/current authorization. TRAFFIC_AUTHORIZE binds explicit current traffic-release human and separately original authorization lineage. Consumer operation is server-derived from registered route/stage, not free client property. Scope/profile proof requirements apply to each initiating actor; service cannot substitute policy read/grant. The fence ends after durable effect admission, not after remote installation; executor performs registered action with exact effect token, records outcome and rechecks next-step admission. Revocation after an already started effect produces stop/reconcile/quarantine as needed, not a false claim the past effect was prevented. UNCHANGED_BUILD recovery/emergency does not invent a new GO dependency; it revalidates current accepted baseline and approved operational source mandates on the same admission boundaries.

## paragraph:69

SUBMITTED→AUTHORIZED creates immutable authorization and outbox atomically, but does not dispatch automatically: explicit dispatch command requires ops.deployment.dispatch, registered executor and current original authority plus receipt. Expired/revoked authorization before dispatch returns409 AUTHORIZATION_NOT_CURRENT and job remains SUBMITTED via append event, requiring fresh authorize revision. Old auth body remains immutable. Reauthorize only no step effect outstanding, or same exact remaining-step plan with successful immutable receipts bound; changed plan new revision/review. An EFFECT_UNKNOWN step cannot be evaded by new job on same environment/component intent; unique unresolved execution barrier blocks it.

## paragraph:70

8 Yürütücü alındısı gerçek etki ve retry

## paragraph:71

OPS-008. ExecutionIntent={intentId:UUID,jobRef,planHash,authorizationRef,stepEffectIds:[{stepId:UUID,effectId:UUID}],generation,commandId,payloadHash,createdAt}. effectId is server stable per job lineage+step semantic identity, never regenerated on HTTP retry, worker restart, restored database or receipt retention expiry. Permanent ledger unique(environmentRootId,generation,intentId,stepId), unique(effectId); tombstone retained under controlled policy and restored consistently. Removed response410 does not permit a new effect. Adapter must support same effectId dedup/query; unavailable capability disables destructive execution, not best-effort retry.

## paragraph:72

Dispatch transaction authorizes exact remaining unsent steps, writes durable intent/outbox. ExecutorReceipt={id,revisionId,intentId,stepId,effectId,attemptNo:int1..100000,executorRef:RevisionRef,acceptedAt:Instant,status:ACCEPTED|REJECTED,rejectionDisposition:NEVER_ACCEPTED_NO_EFFECT|UNKNOWN|null,reasonCode,receiptHash:Hash}. ACCEPTED requires rejectionDisposition=null. REJECTED requires NEVER_ACCEPTED_NO_EFFECT or UNKNOWN; only registered executor signed admission guarantee NEVER_ACCEPTED_NO_EFFECT proves no operation started. Owner adapter atomically derives immutable FINISHED-equivalent StepReceipt FAILED/NONE from that rejection, with no fabricated start/finish clocks (UNKNOWN reason NOT_EXECUTED), source executor receipt ref and next observationNo. This exact no-effect receipt permits the next attempt under the existing rule; REJECTED/UNKNOWN is EFFECT_UNKNOWN and reconcile-only. Rejection status alone cannot prove no effect. ACCEPTED acknowledges delivery only; job DISPATCHING, not applied. Trusted ExecutionEvent={eventId,intentId,stepId,effectId,attemptNo:int1..100000,sequence:Version,eventType:STARTED|OBSERVED|FINISHED,observedAt:FactClock,observation:StepEffectObservation,signatureEvidenceRef:EvidenceRef,recordedAt}. Replay same event identity/hash same result, divergent hash409 EXECUTOR_EVENT_CONFLICT, quarantine. Only registered service identity mTLS/workload proof plus source schema; human cannot post a fabricated executor success through public route.

## paragraph:73

StepEffectObservation={effectState:CONFIRMED|FAILED|UNKNOWN,effectDisposition:NONE|PARTIAL|COMPLETE|UNKNOWN,observedArtifactSetHash:Hash|null,observedConfigHash:Hash|null,observedSchemaHash:Hash|null,affectedComponentIds:UUID[0..100],evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[0..20],reasonCode:Code}. CONFIRMED requires COMPLETE, exact mandatory observations and actual effect evidence; FAILED can be NONE or PARTIAL, never implicitly no effect; UNKNOWN requires UNKNOWN disposition. STARTED/OBSERVED events do not finish step. FINISHED persists immutable StepReceipt={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,version:Version,contentHash:Hash,stepId:UUID,effectId:UUID,attemptNo:int1..100000,observationNo:Version,supersedesReceiptRef:RevisionRef|null,executorReceiptRef:RevisionRef,startedAt:FactClock,finishedAt:FactClock,observation:StepEffectObservation,recordedAt:Instant}. Job EXECUTED_CONFIRMED only all required steps CONFIRMED with exact hashes; any partial/failed yields EXECUTED_FAILED, any outstanding ambiguity EFFECT_UNKNOWN. Actual effect receipt is not independently verified release.

## paragraph:74

Effect ledger stores immutable attempts and observations. Initial attemptNo=1; retry authorized only FAILED/NONE produces next attemptNo under same effectId and new dispatch receipt, preserving previous receipt. Reconcile of UNKNOWN produces a new observationNo on the same attempt with supersedesReceiptRef; never edits old FINISHED body/hash. CurrentStepEffectPointer={effectId,attemptNo,observationNo,currentReceiptRef,version} changes by CAS only from unresolved/definite-no-effect to later proved outcome. CONFIRMED terminal cannot downgrade to FAILED/UNKNOWN or start a new attempt. Contradictory late signed evidence quarantines a discrepancy and blocks current use, while original receipt remains unchanged. Late success for an earlier supposedly no-effect attempt blocks any later admission and reconciles actual effect once; no second successful application accepted silently. Executor rejects overlapping attempts for one effectId and must prove stable no-effect before retry.

## paragraph:75

Retry allowed only same effectId from immutable FAILED/NONE StepReceipt, either an actual FINISHED no-effect observation or the signed NEVER_ACCEPTED_NO_EFFECT rejection-derived receipt described above, under current original human authorization. FAILED/PARTIAL needs new corrective step/authorized plan with predecessor receipt; no replay allsteps. UNKNOWN only reconcile reads adapter by effectId, no dispatch retry. Late success accepted monotonically into original receipt lineage, does not fabricate new authorization. Cancel only DRAFT/SUBMITTED or AUTHORIZED with all steps NEVER_DISPATCHED under dispatch lock, creates CANCELLED and revokes auth. Once accepted/unknown, cancel is STOP_REQUESTED operational signal; public stop command produces request receipt and conservative traffic block, not CANCELLED or proof of stopped process. Existing successful steps are immutable, reused by reference in superseding job; new plan maps reusedStepReceiptRef+exact effect/hash and cannot rerun it.

## paragraph:76

Executor can perform only submitted allowlisted plan and stage-specific adapter capabilities. A changed payload, command, environment generation, credential grant or secret inventory revision cannot reuse old authorization. Immediately before each side effect registered executor obtains current DispatchFence binding effectId+operationRef+auth+source+generation; no source access means stop before unsent effect. If source becomes unavailable during effect, preserve UNKNOWN and block traffic until reconcile. Error text must redact secrets, network addresses and raw SQL. Support cannot inspect executor auth material.

9 Dağıtım doğrulaması ve trafik açılması

## paragraph:78

OPS-009. DeploymentVerifyDto={executionReceiptRef:RevisionRef,postDeployAssessmentRef:RevisionRef,decision:VALID|INVALID|INCONCLUSIVE,reason:Text10..2000,evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[1..20]}. Verify actor not maker, authorizer or executor and must hold selector-specific action plus ops.execution.verify qualification. VALID requires immutable all-step CONFIRMED, observed artifact/config/schema equals plan, full current POST_DEPLOY assessment PASS. INVALID/INCONCLUSIVE preserve actual effect and job blocked; create new verification receipt but no data rollback assumed. VERIFIED is technical acceptance, traffic remains closed unless independently released.

TrafficReleaseDto={verificationRef:RevisionRef,currentAssessmentRef:RevisionRef,expectedGeneration:UUID,reason:Text10..2000}. ops.deployment.traffic_release AND ops.release_authority, independent from execution verifier, current whole-source fence and exact service impact mandate. Creates ReleaseAuthorizationRecord={id,revisionId,purpose:TRAFFIC_OPEN|RECOVERY_RESUME,jobRef,jobKind:DEPLOYMENT|RECOVERY,environmentRef,generation,verifiedExecutionReceiptRef,verificationRef,assessmentRef,participantCheckpointSetHash:Hash|null,allowedParticipantKinds:ParticipantKind[],authorizedByPersonId,mandateRef,sourceVectorHash,issuedAt,validUntil,epoch,state:ACTIVE|REVOKED|EXPIRED|CONSUMED}. These are the only refs called releaseAuthorizationRef by consumers; Deployment execution authorization has a distinct purpose/type. TRAFFIC_OPEN allowedParticipantKinds=[]; it cannot resume restored mobile/integration ledgers.

## paragraph:80

Release command emits explicit OPEN_TRAFFIC intent after release authorization commit; actual traffic state={desired:CLOSED|OPEN,observed:CLOSED|OPEN|UNKNOWN,receiptRef:null|RevisionRef}. Job RELEASED only registered gateway effect CONFIRMED and independent runtime observation matches, not immediately on authorization. Grace period cannot hide missing monitoring. OPEN_TRAFFIC unknown means traffic state UNKNOWN, conservative HOLD for new execution. Closing traffic command always specific authorized operational safety action with receipt, not guarantee network effect. Critical isolation/auth/business smoke failure records conservative STOP/HOLD and requests close; verification cannot claim rollback happened until receipt. Source incident links to63.06/61.01 do not release vessel HOLD.

OPS-010. RecoveryJob={id,version,environmentRef,scope,sourceGeneration:UUID,targetGeneration:UUID,reviewState:NOT_STARTED|IN_REVIEW|APPROVED|RETURNED|REJECTED,planRevisionRef:RevisionRef,state:DRAFT|DECLARED|AUTHORIZED|ISOLATED_RUNNING|ISOLATED_UNKNOWN|ISOLATED_FAILED|ISOLATED_VERIFIED|TARGET_RUNNING|TARGET_UNKNOWN|TARGET_FAILED|RECONCILING|VERIFIED|RESUME_AUTHORIZED|RESUMED|CANCELLED|CLOSED,mode:EXERCISE|ACTUAL,incidentRef:RevisionRef|null,plan:RecoveryPlan,planHash,authorizationRef:null|RevisionRef,isolatedReceiptRef:null|RevisionRef,targetReceiptRef:null|RevisionRef,reconciliationRef:null|RevisionRef,releaseAuthorizationRef:null|RevisionRef,createdByPersonId,createdAt}. ## paragraph:83

RecoveryPlan={backupPointRef:RevisionRef,expectedBackupSetHash:Hash,targetEnvironmentRef:EnvironmentRef,isolatedEnvironmentRef:EnvironmentRef,restoreBoundary:FactClock,affectedComponentIds:UUID[1..100],procedureRef:RunbookRef,expectedArtifactSetHash:Hash,expectedConfigHash:Hash,expectedSchemaHash:Hash,acceptedBuildBaselineRef:RevisionRef,participants:ParticipantSpec[1..100],steps:ExecutionStep[1..100],window:{from,to},dataLossAssessmentRef:RevisionRef,reason:Text10..2000}. Same accepted build recovery does not require new GO decision; baseline accepted manifest/promotion receipt current and restoration procedure/config compatible. Any new artifact/schema plan requires separate65.02 NEW_RELEASE acceptance and deployment authorization, not recovery bypass. Recovery point may be older but current hold/auth/receipt overlay cannot roll back authority.

## paragraph:84

Declaration current ops.recovery.declare AND ops.incident.command mandate creates RecoveryBarrier={id,environmentRootId,sourceGeneration,targetGeneration,affectedComponentIds,barrierEpoch:Epoch,state:PAUSING|PAUSED|RESTORING|RECONCILING|RESUME_AUTHORIZED|CLOSED,participantSetHash,controlJournalRef:RevisionRef}. Normal changes/dispatch stop immediately at epoch check; actual writer/queue pause confirmed only participant receipts. A durable control journal and current authorization/revocation witness must be outside the data being overwritten, on approved on-prem/self-hosted protected control plane or independently recoverable signed store. No mandatory cloud/region service. If latest journal/auth witness unavailable, restore control authorization cannot be reconstructed from stale backup; fail closed. Manual disaster narrative after system returns is reported fact, not backdated positive authority.

## paragraph:85

RecoveryAuthorizeDto={planHash,backupVerificationRef:RevisionRef,requiredPauseSetRef:RevisionRef,validUntil,reason}. Current recovery commander authorizes after independent operations approver and security approver have approved same immutable plan, neither creator nor executor; public review commands separated by slot. Every required source participant pause receipt confirmed and current writes fenced before restore starts. Source runtime restore lock plus barrier generation prevents ordinary commands against old generation. Isolated execution with original effect IDs tests actual restore into approved isolated target, never overwrites production first. ISOLATED_VERIFIED needs independent full RESTORE_COMPLETENESS/AUTH_TENANT/DATA_PROVENANCE/KEY_RECOVERY plus matching measured report, not backup success.

## paragraph:86

Apply-target command after ISOLATED_VERIFIED rechecks current recovery authorization, exact tested backup/plan hash, full pause set and new generation fence; emits distinct target step effect identity. Failed isolated test blocks apply-target. Target apply does not open traffic, queues, key unwrap or login acceptance automatically. TARGET_UNKNOWN reconcile only. TARGET_FAILED/PARTIAL needs corrective recovery plan, never restart same write with new intent. Success enters RECONCILING; domain ledgers and current revocation/hold overlays reconcile before VERIFIED. Cancel is allowed only before first dispatch and confirmed all participant pause release safely handled; if pause effect unresolved recovery remains declared/barrier active.

11 Katılımcı checkpoint ve tekrar güvenliği

## paragraph:88

OPS-011. ParticipantKind=CORE_DATABASE|AUTH_REVOCATION|EVIDENCE_CUSTODY|AUDIT_EFFECT_LEDGER|INTEGRATION_LEDGER|MOBILE_CUSTODY|ONBOARDING_SOURCE. ## paragraph:89

RecoveryParticipantPortV1 trusted internal request={barrierRef:RevisionRef,environmentRef,sourceGeneration,targetGeneration,participantKind,expectedParticipantContractRef,originalActorContext:TrustedActorContext,purpose:PAUSE|VERIFY_CHECKPOINT|PREPARE_RESUME}. Response union PAUSE_RECEIPT {ref,barrierRef,participantKind,owner,sourceGeneration,state:PAUSED|UNKNOWN|FAILED,pausedAt:FactClock,writerFenceEpoch,sourceCheckpointHash:Hash|null}; CHECKPOINT {ref,barrierRef,participantKind,targetGeneration,checkpointHash:Hash,verificationRef:RevisionRef,state:VERIFIED|BLOCKED|UNKNOWN,authEpoch,sourceEpoch,validUntil}; PREPARED {ref,checkpointRef,releaseAuthorizationRef,participantEpoch,validUntil} or BLOCKED {reasonCode,checkedAt}. Read-only verification does not pause as side effect: PAUSE is explicitly authorized internal command, its own stable effectId supplied in TrustedConsumerCommitContext. Every mutation has owner durable receipt; same receipt rules apply.

## paragraph:90

Adapter registration binds actual owner operations, not an invented universal endpoint. 65.01 CORE/AUDIT adapter fences DB maintenance writers and durable command/effect journal; 52.02/54.02 adapter restores authentication state only with latest authoritative revocation overlay, no old grant resurrection;58.02 adapter validates evidence hashes plus current hold/disposition and key custody;58.01 adapter approved source manifests/import effect map;63.02 adapter current connector outbox/import receipts/canonical identities;63.03 adapter frozen MobileRestoreCheckpoint. Owner that has not accepted this port remains disabled at integration gate. Adapter registration and deployment fixture is a required implementation deliverable, not a claim it already exists. No alternate direct source table read/write is allowed when an adapter is unavailable.

## paragraph:91

63.03 MobileRestoreCheckpoint exact source fields id,deploymentGeneration,deviceRegistryCheckpoint,authRevocationCheckpoint,policyCheckpoint,queueIntentCheckpoint,ownerReceiptCheckpointManifest,evidenceCustodyCheckpoint,keyAvailabilityManifestHash,verifiedAt,state BLOCKED|VERIFIED,verificationEvidenceRef are consumed without changing source schema. source deploymentGeneration=targetGeneration. All original command/journal/effect tombstones and custody encryption key availability must match restored owner receipts; partial checkpoint UNKNOWN. Mobile source producer verification is not human traffic authorization. ResumeMobileDispatch exact command {checkpointId,checkpointHash,releaseAuthorizationRef,expectedDeploymentGeneration}; adapter derives ids/hash from source checkpoint, not arbitrary admin toggle.

## paragraph:92

RecoveryReconciliation={id,revisionId,jobRef,barrierRef,participantSetHash,entries:[{kind,checkpointRef,checkpointHash,verificationRef,sourceEpoch}][1..100],unresolvedEffects:KNOWN_ZERO|PRESENT|UNKNOWN,duplicatePrevention:VERIFIED|BLOCKED|UNKNOWN,holdOverlay:VERIFIED|BLOCKED|UNKNOWN,revocationOverlay:VERIFIED|BLOCKED|UNKNOWN,sourceVectorHash,verifiedByPersonId,verifiedAt,validUntil}. Entries exactly complete required set, known zero only full ledger reconciliation, never visible filtered count. Any pending/unknown external effect remains quarantine. Already committed effects retain immutable receipts and separate current validity; restore cannot mark them unsent. Consumer new source generation requires source owner read/revalidation but preserves permanent intent identity. Deleted response/tombstone cannot become retry permission.

## paragraph:93

RecoveryVerifyDto={targetReceiptRef,reconciliationRef,assessmentRef,measurementCheckRef,decision:VALID|INVALID|INCONCLUSIVE,reason,evidenceRefs}. Independent verify requires actual target CONFIRMED, full current RECOVERY_RESUME assessment PASS, complete reconciliation and correct source-generation cutover; measurement UNKNOWN does not become a fabricated objective PASS. If operational objective requires quantified RPO/RTO, unknown measurement blocks positive verification; exercise-only receipt can remain verified restoration integrity with objective UNKNOWN but cannot satisfy full production readiness slot. VERIFIED state means gates met, not traffic opened.

## paragraph:94

Resume-authorize explicit independent ops.recovery.resume_authorize AND ops.release_authority creates purpose RECOVERY_RESUME ReleaseAuthorizationRecord with exact participantCheckpointSetHash and allowedParticipantKinds full required set. Then per-owner same-transaction WithReleaseAuthorizationFence binds source checkpoint/current generation, original authority and authorized effectId through owner resume commit. No read proof followed by unguarded queue enable. Cross-owner operations are not assumed atomically distributed: prepare all exact current checkpoints, enable one by one under each fence, record per-participant RESUMED|BLOCKED|UNKNOWN receipt. Traffic remains CLOSED until every required resume receipt CONFIRMED plus current health proof; a partial failure leaves barrier RECONCILING, never overall RESUMED. Already resumed owner can be explicitly paused again under same barrier; failure to pause is UNKNOWN and new work admission remains conservative. Source business current gate is still mandatory after queue starts.

## paragraph:95

Open-traffic for recovery requires all participant resume receipts CONFIRMED at same generation, current independent verification and release authorization. EXERCISE cannot authorize production resume. Job RESUMED only confirmed traffic receipt; close requires post-recovery incident reconciliation/RCA links and no unknown effects, but retains barrier/checkpoint history. Restore does not delete security incident, autoextend session, reset grace period or overwrite active legal hold. New failure creates new immutable recovery/incident entry, not editing history to successful.

OPS-012. ServiceIncident={id,version,environmentRef,scope,severity:P0|P1|P2|P3,severityAssessment:PENDING|ASSESSED,cycleNo:int1..100000,state:DETECTED|TRIAGED|CONTAINMENT_PENDING|MITIGATING|RECOVERING|VERIFICATION_PENDING|RESOLVED|CLOSED|REOPENED,summary:Text10..200,description:Text10..4000,detectedAt:FactClock,recordedAt,commanderAssignmentRef:RevisionRef|null,assigneeAssignmentRef:RevisionRef|null,releaseContextRef:RevisionRef|null,signalRefs:RevisionRef[0..100],relatedDomainIncidentRefs:RevisionRef[0..20],actions:ServiceActionRef[0..100],responseObjectiveRef:EnvironmentRef|null,responseDueAt:Instant|null,firstHumanResponseAt:Instant|null,resolutionVerificationRef:RevisionRef|null,problemRef:RevisionRef|null,closedAt:Instant|null}. ## paragraph:98

TriageDto={severity,impact:Impact,commanderAssignmentRef:null|RevisionRef,assigneeAssignmentRef:RevisionRef,reason:Text10..2000,relatedDomainIncidentRefs:RevisionRef[0..20]}. Impact={availability:TOTAL|PARTIAL|NONE|UNKNOWN,dataIntegrity:CONFIRMED_RISK|NO_KNOWN_RISK|UNKNOWN,tenantIsolation:CONFIRMED_RISK|NO_KNOWN_RISK|UNKNOWN,safetySignal:CONFIRMED_RISK|NO_KNOWN_RISK|UNKNOWN,affectedComponentIds:UUID[0..100],knownFrom:FactClock}. Confirmed safety/data/security/isolation or total outage gives server minimum P0; no known risk is not proof of safety. P0/P1 commander current52.01 assignment + ops.incident.command mandatory; technical admin label no mandate. Severity downgrade is separate command requiring independent current review/evidence; unknown impact cannot justify lower severity. Response target stored at detection from approved profile; later profile changes do not rewrite historical dueAt. Missing business calendar or objective yields dueAt=null/UNKNOWN.

## paragraph:99

ServiceActionRef={kind:DEPLOYMENT|RECOVERY|CYBER_CONTAINMENT|ACCESS_REQUEST|NOTIFICATION,ref:RevisionRef,requestState:REQUESTED|ACKNOWLEDGED|REJECTED|UNKNOWN,effectState:CONFIRMED|FAILED|UNKNOWN|NOT_APPLICABLE}. Incident link command only attaches existing source refs; it does not execute them. CONTAINMENT_PENDING may become MITIGATING only actor records current source receipt, not because request sent. ResolveDto={verificationCheckRef:RevisionRef,remainingRisk:Text10..2000,problemRef:null|RevisionRef,communicationReceiptRef:null|RevisionRef}; requires environment current health/technical correction verification independent of implementer. Unknown effect cannot RESOLVED. Close requires RESOLVED, no unresolved critical actions, P0/P1 problem+RCA record and exact linked owner task acceptance, not merely task creation.

## paragraph:100

ReopenDto={reason:Text10..2000,newSignalRefs:RevisionRef[1..20]}; CLOSED/RESOLVED→REOPENED retains earlier resolution/close receipts, new response cycle tracked by cycleNo, not resetting original metrics. Ordinary factual correction via appendCorrection {field:SUMMARY|DESCRIPTION|DETECTED_CLOCK|IMPACT_NOTE,correctedValue:typed corresponding field,reason,evidenceRefs}; correction cannot alter signed severity/verification/assignment or erase history. Duplicate incident linking requires current scope and verified same event; no merge deletion. Service problem={id,version,summary:Text10..200,incidentRefs[1..100],state:OPEN|ANALYZED|ACTION_TRACKING|VERIFIED|CLOSED,rootCause:Text0..4000,correctiveTaskRefs:RevisionRef[0..100] owner54.03,knowledgeRevisionRef:RunbookRef|null,verificationCheckRef:null|RevisionRef}. Task completion alone not technical fix proof; independent check required. Knowledge workaround expiry in58.02, not an unbounded support note.

External customer communications are not implicit incident effects. PreparedCommunication={id,incidentRef,factsHash,approvedRecipientSetRef:RevisionRef,purpose:OUTAGE_UPDATE|RECOVERY_UPDATE,contentEvidenceRef:EvidenceRef,approvedByPersonId,validUntil,notificationReceiptRef:RevisionRef|null owner58.01}. V1 service case stores only existing approved communication/delivery refs via action-link; compose/send stays58.01 current audience/content permission. Delivery RECEIVED is not customer read/acknowledged. Legal notice decisions remain63.06; service severity does not invent legal deadline or recipient. UI shows verified known facts and planned next-update time separately, no forecast as fact.

External upload/send not part of diagnostic collect. V1 package download is same authenticated explicitly authorized recipient through58.02 current read/export permission and exact purpose; external recipient without approved authenticated read route cannot receive via arbitrary URL. Recipient change new request/approval; expired original ticket no silent resend. ## paragraph:105

DiagnosticAuthorizeDto={requestHash:Hash,recipientHash:Hash,fieldMask:DiagnosticFieldPath[1..50],redactionPolicyRef:RunbookRef,expiresAt:Instant,reason:Text10..2000}. Independent privacy/security approver not requester/collector/recipient, action ops.diagnostic.authorize AND ops.diagnostic.disclosure_authority; current source grant must cover every selected category/row field. DiagnosticFieldPath closed schema registry enumerates safe errorCode,correlationId,screenId,actionKey,serverAt,networkState,buildHash,configHash,queueDisposition,receiptId,connectorCode,latencyMs,resourcePercent,redactedMessage,evidenceRef. Host/IP/username/full payload/SQL/text trace not included. Raw inputs are server source-local temporary buffers under approved policy, never queued/exported before redaction; no arbitrary regex requested by client.

## paragraph:106

Collection operation registered source adapter performs exact authorized field allowlist and privacy redaction, creates DiagnosticPackageManifest={id,revisionId,requestRef,requestHash,scope,sourceManifestHash,categoryCodes,fieldMaskHash,redactionPolicyRef,redactionReportRef,packageEvidenceRef:EvidenceRef,packageHash:Hash,scan:CLEAN|PENDING|BLOCKED,contentState:REVIEW_REQUIRED|APPROVED|REJECTED,expiresAt}. CLEAN malware status not approval. Independent content-review approver verifies deterministic redaction report plus sampled exact manifest and source access; failed/unreadable source or scan pending stays INCONCLUSIVE/REVIEW_REQUIRED. Package storage/read/hold/disposition stays58.02. Collector success does not set READY until contentStateAPPROVED and recipient/current grant check. Binary is never embedded in generic health/incident read.

## paragraph:107

Download-request command checks same recipient/purpose, exact READY package hash, expiry, source current access,58.02 hold/read and current disclosure authority; returns owner-controlled download ticket descriptor, never long-lived bare URL. Revocation/expiry closes future read, event logged; already downloaded external copy cannot be claimed deleted. Source loss while collecting aborts/quarantines package, not partial “complete” pack. Audit records request/approval/source/fieldmask/recipient hash and ticket receipt, not secret or body. Reproduction screenshots must use58.02CLEAN evidence with redaction review; no automatic screen recording, location capture or hidden remote access.

OPS-014. SupportAccessIntent={id,version,supersedesIntentRef:RevisionRef|null,incidentRef,environmentRef,scope,personRef:RevisionRef owner52.01,organizationRef:RevisionRef|null,purpose:DIAGNOSTIC_READ|REGISTERED_RECOVERY_OPERATION|REGISTERED_EMERGENCY_CHANGE,requestedActionKeys:ActionKey[1..20],requestedFieldMask:Code[0..50],validFrom:Instant,validUntil:Instant,reason:Text20..2000,planRef:RevisionRef|null,state:DRAFT|REQUESTED|APPROVED|REJECTED|IAM_PENDING|ACTIVE|REVOKE_REQUESTED|REVOKED|EXPIRED|CLOSED,approvalRefs:RevisionRef[0..2],iamAccessRequestRef:RevisionRef|null,iamGrantReceiptRef:RevisionRef|null,sessionReceiptRefs:RevisionRef[0..100]}. ## paragraph:110

Two independent votes OPERATIONS and SECURITY required on exact intent hash; different people, neither target/requester; exact selector action plus current mandate. IAM link command attaches existing52.02/54.02AccessRequestRef and immutable current source grant receipt through registered IAM_ACCESS_REFERENCE_V1 adapter. No new IAM route guessed here. Current adapter read response={requestRef,requestState:PENDING|APPROVED|REJECTED|CANCELLED,grantReceiptRef:null|RevisionRef,grantState:NONE|PENDING|ACTIVE|REVOKED|EXPIRED|UNKNOWN,principalPersonId,scopeHash,actionSetHash,fieldMaskHash,validFrom,validUntil,sourceEpoch}. Fields must match intent exactly or narrower, never wider; source ACTIVE still requires actual actor current session/action grant at use. Adapter not registered→IAM_PENDING/UNKNOWN, not ACTIVE.

## paragraph:111

Revocation request creates65.01REVOKE_REQUESTED and source IAM safe handoff; actual REVOKED only owner receipt confirms. Expiry current check immediately blocks new operational use even if worker delayed or IAM status stale. It does not claim remote process terminated or credential deleted. Usage log references exact session/action receipts and incident; access closure requires expired/revoked grant plus no unknown effects and independent usage review. No automatic renew: new intent/new independent approval, same unresolved effect cannot escape through new access window. Emergency label never bypasses source proof, participant checkpoint, privacy export or formal medical/financial authority. Real credentials entered only by existing approved IAM/secret-store workflows; this module never stores them.

OPS-015. HealthSignal={id,revisionId,environmentRef,componentId,aggregation:LAST|AVG|P50|P95|SUM|RATE,kind:API_AVAILABILITY|API_LATENCY|API_ERRORS|DB_CAPACITY_LOCKS|BACKUP_PITR|MOBILE_QUEUE|EVIDENCE_INTEGRITY|AUTH_FAILURES|CONNECTOR_EFFECTS|BUSINESS_SAFETY|JOB_LAG|INFRA_CAPACITY,window:{from,to},sampleCount:int≥0|null,coverage:COMPLETE|PARTIAL|UNKNOWN,value:MetricValue,assessment:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN,thresholdRef:EnvironmentRef,collectorRef:RevisionRef,recordedAt,validUntil,sourceEpoch}. DecimalString^-?[0-9]{1,15}(\.[0-9]{1,6})?$; latency/count not negative; percent0..100. Empty sample is UNKNOWN, zero is observed value, null not0. Threshold policy exact approved check procedure/environmentref, arbitrary AI health score yok.

## paragraph:114

Health ingest internal service only, immutable collector sequence/idempotency, registered metric schema and environment/component allowlist. Collector heartbeat not business readiness. Source-specific health such as63.02connector current effect status and63.03queue metadata is owner-projected, not raw database polling. Generic health metric row is disclosed only if every contributing row and all projected/predicate fields are currently readable and the viewer has ops.health.aggregate.read for this exact aggregate scope. Otherwise the entire HealthRow is omitted, including signalRef,assessment,coverage,timestamps,value and link; no variable placeholder or hidden row count. Internal stored MetricValue WITHHELD cannot be serialized as an otherwise informative public row. HealthSummary becomes UNKNOWN/allnull when full population is not disclosable; private omissions do not alter the required set used internally. No account names, IPs, vulnerabilities, access-token error bodies, medical or crew narratives in generic dashboard.

QueryBase={companyId:UUID,environmentId:UUID,cursor?:Text2048,pageSize?:int1..100}; environmentId root current effective selection at read start, exact selected revision in response. Default25/max100; cursor signed principal+scope+allfilters+sort+immutable snapshot/source vector+auth/env epochs,TTL300s. Current-only views, no undeclared asOf query. Changed source/cursor context409 CURSOR_CONTEXT_CHANGED; unknown fields400, scope mismatch403/404. No-store, protected browser cache disabled;63.03 offline profile not registered for these screens. ReadEnvelope<T>={schemaVersion:OPS_READ_V1,evaluatedAt:Instant,validUntil:Instant,environmentRef:EnvironmentRef|null,sourceState:CURRENT|PARTIAL|UNAVAILABLE,sourceFenceHash:Hash|null,data:T,permittedActions:ActionKey[0..30]}. CURRENT validUntil=min(evaluatedAt+60s,sourcebounds),PARTIAL no positive overall; UNAVAILABLE validUntil=evaluatedAt,environmentRef only if safely authorized metadata known,sourceFenceHashnull. permittedActions server exact actions, not grant tokens. Page<T>={items:T[0..100],nextCursor:Text2048|null}; no total by default. Counts only typedSummary KNOWN full-set+aggregate grant or UNKNOWN/allnull. Empty authorized items never no-issue proof.

Query=QueryBase+{componentKind?:ComponentEntry.kind,signalKind?:HealthSignal.kind,assessment?:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN,aggregation?:LAST|AVG|P50|P95|SUM|RATE}. Data={signals:Page<HealthRow>,summary:HealthSummary,links:SafeLink[0..10]}. HealthRow={rowId:UUID,signalRef:RevisionRef,componentCode:Code,componentKind,signalKind,assessment,evaluatedAt:Instant,validUntil:Instant,value:MetricValue,coverage:COMPLETE|PARTIAL|UNKNOWN,link:SafeLink|null}; rowId=signalRevisionId; HealthRow additionally aggregation:MetricThreshold.aggregation, stable sort componentCodeASC,signalKindASC,aggregationASC,rowIdASC. HealthSummary={state:OWNER_REPORTED|UNKNOWN,passCount:int|null,failCount:int|null,unknownCount:int|null,coverage:COMPLETE|PARTIAL|UNKNOWN,lastCollectionAt:Instant|null}. Filtering by assessment,signalKind,componentKind or aggregation requires current field/predicate rights for the whole queried source scope before execution; unauthorized protected predicate403, never filtering hidden rows to reveal their existence. Counts full current selected signal population with same filters, never page sum; separate ops.health.aggregate.read qualification plus all source contributors required. Any hidden contributor returns allnull/UNKNOWN, no hidden cardinality.

Query=QueryBase+{purpose:INSTALL_ACCEPTANCE|PRE_DEPLOY|POST_DEPLOY|RECOVERY_ISOLATED|RECOVERY_RESUME,jobKind?:DEPLOYMENT|RECOVERY,jobId?:UUID}; jobKind/jobId together, context purpose strict matching, PRE/POST deployment requiresDEPLOYMENT, recovery purposesRECOVERY; install neither. Query does not evaluate/create assessment. ReadinessRow={rowId:UUID,slotCode:CheckKind,componentCode:Code|null,result:PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN|NOT_APPLICABLE,sourceRef:RevisionRef|null,evaluatedAt:Instant,validUntil:Instant,link:SafeLink|null}; sort slotCodeASC,componentCodenulllast,rowIdASC. Full assessment-source read required for PASS; unreadable slot causes ownerOverallUNKNOWN and no hidden row or reason count.

## paragraph:119

ReleaseAcceptanceView={kind:NOT_APPLICABLE} for installation/same-build recovery, {kind:AVAILABLE,candidateRef,decisionRef,decision:GO|CONDITIONAL|FIX|NO_GO,currentUse:VALID|BLOCKED|UNKNOWN,link:SafeLink} for current authorized65.02 read, or {kind:UNAVAILABLE,reasonCode:SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE|NOT_AUTHORIZED|NOT_CONFIGURED}. NO source proof token in read view. Screen primary action current refresh, secondary owner links to relevant check/candidate/job/incident only. “Ready” is owner assessment label and separate software acceptance, not deploy button or new approval. All mutations occur explicit owner resource screens and exact action. Read projection cannot create assessment, authorize release or start restore as a side effect.

## paragraph:220

Environment retirement closureCheckRef must be VALID ENVIRONMENT_HEALTH special procedure RETIREMENT proving no serving traffic, active grants requiring system, unresolved execution/custody or pending retention migration; retirement never deletes backups/evidence. SUSPENDED root can activate a newly independently approved revision; no direct unsuspend bypass. REJECTED environment/check material cannot be edited: new revision via original root revise or new check with supersedesCheckRef required; CheckCreateDto additionally supersedesCheckRef:RevisionRef|null, same kind/context, immutable lineage. New valid correction explicitly supersedes prior failed check; no arbitrary old PASS selection.

## paragraph:271

Deployment emergency Review APPROVE adds current slot receipt; REJECT moves submitted job REJECTED, RETURN creates successor DRAFT plan revision and invalidates previous review set. No current successful or unknown effect may be hidden by this path. Rejected plan may be revised via new deployment create with supersedesJobRef:RevisionRef|null; same unresolved intent barrier still applies. DeploymentCreateDto includes supersedesJobRef and reusedStepRefs:RevisionRef[0..100], each exact successful prior step bound to unchanged semantic step identity. No public direct step receipt create exists. Manual operator facts are separate OperationalCheck evidence; cannot masquerade as executor event. ExecutionAuthorize validUntil cannot exceed approved window/current proof validity; source freshness rechecked at dispatch and per-step. Emergency unchanged-build iki ReviewDto oyu aşağıdaki deployment emergency-decision actionlarını kullanır; recovery review izni bu rota için geçmez. UNCHANGED_BUILD requires both APPROVE receipts for planHash before authorize, other branches reject such votes as inapplicable.

## paragraph:371

Recovery Review RETURN cannot drop a live barrier: plan state DECLARED with reviewState=RETURNED, new plan draft child revision and source fence; barrier stays PAUSED until safe cancel or new fully approved plan. REJECT likewise reviewState=REJECTED, no target execution; authorized plan requires all votes APPROVE current. RecoveryJob therefore stores reviewState:NOT_STARTED|IN_REVIEW|APPROVED|RETURNED|REJECTED and planRevisionRef; draft edit after declaration targets new child revision under barrier, never mutates old plan hash. ISOLATED/target FAILED retry has no generic public redo: reconcile first, then new corrective plan revision with successful reusedStepRefs and original permanent effect ledger, repeat both independent votes. Target apply is explicitly disallowed EXERCISE unless target is the declared isolated environment; no real production mutation from test.

## paragraph:479

SupportAccessRequestDto exact material fields from SupportAccessIntent: incidentRef,environmentRef,scope,personRef,organizationRef,purpose,requestedActionKeys,requestedFieldMask,validFrom,validUntil,reason,planRef. RETURN creates successor DRAFT intent revision; POST /support-access new request additionally supersedesIntentRef:RevisionRef|null, does not copy old votes or extend old grant. Diagnostic correction/renewal likewise new request with supersedesRequestRef:RevisionRef|null; same unresolved collection effect must reconcile before recollect, same immutable package reused only if hash/source/recipient approval unchanged. Incident initial severity is P0 with severityAssessment=PENDING|ASSESSED, not undeclared severity enum; current unknown impact conservative P0 until triage. REOPENED→TRIAGED fresh triage. Stage progress guards: CONTAINMENT_PENDING requires typed source request; MITIGATING requires current acknowledged action/effect status not assumed contained; RECOVERING requires authorized recovery/deployment link; VERIFICATION_PENDING requires execution receipt, unresolved UNKNOWN remains blocking resolve.

## paragraph:563

ReceiptView strict union={kind,receiptRef:RevisionRef,subjectRef:RevisionRef,recordedAt:Instant,sourceState:CURRENT|STALE|UNKNOWN,content:KindSpecificReceipt}. Closed map: EXECUTOR→ExecutorReceipt (§8 registered executor intake); STEP→StepReceipt (§8 immutable observation lineage); VERIFICATION→VerificationReceipt (§5 independent owner verify, extended below); APPROVAL→ReviewReceipt; PAUSE→PauseReceipt; PAUSE_SET→PauseSetRevision (§18 declaration resolver); CHECKPOINT→ParticipantCheckpoint; RECONCILIATION→RecoveryReconciliation (§11 current full-set resolver); TRAFFIC→TrafficEffectReceipt; RESUME→ParticipantResumeReceipt; DIAGNOSTIC_COLLECTION→DiagnosticPackageManifest (§13 registered collection producer); IAM_LINK→IamLinkReceipt. No mutation DTO is a receipt schema. Required content field unavailable means whole404/403, no masked body that can be rewritten.

## paragraph:564

VerificationReceipt always contains id,revisionId,subjectRef,subjectHash,requiredSetRef,coverageHash,decision,decisionActorPersonId,mandateAssignmentRef,reason,verifiedAt,validUntil,sourceVectorHash,verificationEpoch as declared§5. Add kind:CHECK|DEPLOYMENT|RECOVERY_ISOLATED|RECOVERY_TARGET,executionReceiptRef:RevisionRef|null,assessmentRef:RevisionRef|null,reconciliationRef:RevisionRef|null,evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[1..50]. CHECK uses required item set/coverage and null execution/assessment/reconciliation unless source context explicitly references them; deployment requires execution+assessment, null reconciliation; isolated recovery requires isolated execution+assessment, target recovery requires all3. requiredSetRef/coverageHash for deployment/recovery come from exact assessment required-set; same subjectHash binds job plan and selected immutable receipt vector. Decision actor is actual human verifier, never callback service committer. Each public verify selector produces this immutable receipt in the same source state transaction.

## paragraph:565

ReviewReceipt={id:UUID,revisionId:UUID,version:Version,contentHash:Hash,subjectRef:RevisionRef,slot:ENGINEERING|PRODUCT|SECURITY|OPERATIONS,decision:APPROVE|REJECT|RETURN,subjectHash:Hash,actorPersonId:UUID,assignmentRef:RevisionRef,mandateRef:RevisionRef,reason:Text10..2000,evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[0..20],recordedAt:Instant,validUntil:Instant,sourceVectorHash:Hash}. Server review-route context fixes allowed slot subset and actual person. PauseReceipt={id,revisionId,barrierRef,participantKind,owner,sourceGeneration,state:PAUSED|UNKNOWN|FAILED,pausedAt:FactClock,writerFenceEpoch:Epoch,sourceCheckpointHash:Hash|null,recordedAt}; ParticipantCheckpoint={id,revisionId,barrierRef,participantKind,targetGeneration,checkpointHash:Hash,verificationRef:RevisionRef,state:VERIFIED|BLOCKED|UNKNOWN,authEpoch:Epoch,sourceEpoch:Epoch,validUntil:Instant,recordedAt}. Pause/checkpoint producer is registered source participant adapter,65.01 stores immutable owner receipt reference and native hash, no human synthetic source success.

## paragraph:566

TrafficEffectReceipt={id,revisionId,effectId:UUID,authorizationRef:RevisionRef,generation:UUID,desired:CLOSED|OPEN,observed:CLOSED|OPEN|UNKNOWN,effectState:CONFIRMED|FAILED|UNKNOWN,effectDisposition:NONE|PARTIAL|COMPLETE|UNKNOWN,evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[1..20],observedAt:FactClock,recordedAt}; registered traffic executor producer. ParticipantResumeReceipt={id,revisionId,effectId,authorizationRef,barrierRef,participantKind,checkpointRef,checkpointHash:Hash,generation,effectState,effectDisposition,evidenceRefs:EvidenceRef[1..20],observedAt:FactClock,recordedAt}; source owner WithReleaseAuthorizationFence callback producer. IamLinkReceipt={id,revisionId,intentRef:RevisionRef,sourceRequestRef:RevisionRef,sourceGrantReceiptRef:RevisionRef|null,sourceProjection:exact IAM_ACCESS_REFERENCE_V1 response,checkedAt:Instant,sourceEpoch:Epoch}; IAM adapter produces current observed linkage, not permission grant. All id/revision/generation fields UUID, version inherited source RevisionRef; receipt hashes canonical material, approval refs not fed back into subject material. Each receipt family enforces its exact owner allowlist and current person/source field rights before read.

## paragraph:567

List state discriminator is fixed by route: environments→Environment.state; backup-points→BackupPoint.state; checks→OperationalCheck.state; assessments→OperationalAssessment.overall; deployments→DeploymentJob.state; recoveries→RecoveryJob.state; incidents→ServiceIncident.state; problems→ServiceProblem.state; diagnostics→DiagnosticRequest.state; support-access→SupportAccessIntent.state. Assessment list row state is overall PASS|FAIL|UNKNOWN, not an undeclared lifecycle. Each resource inherits createdAt:Instant and updatedAt:Instant server metadata; immutable objects updatedAt=createdAt. Environment revision detail binds its root current-read scope. First human triage sets firstHumanResponseAt once per incident cycle with server receivedAt; machine detection or delivery acknowledgement never starts human-response satisfaction.

## paragraph:568

AuditQuery={companyId,environmentId,subjectKind?:ENVIRONMENT|BACKUP|CHECK|ASSESSMENT|DEPLOYMENT|RECOVERY|INCIDENT|PROBLEM|DIAGNOSTIC|SUPPORT_ACCESS,subjectId?:UUID,from:Instant,to:Instant,cursor?,pageSize?};≤31days,subjectId requireskind. AuditRow={eventId:UUID,recordedAt,subjectRef,actionKey,actorType:HUMAN|SERVICE,actorPersonId:UUID|null,commandId:UUID,oldState:Code|null,newState:Code|null,resultCode:Code,correlationId:UUID,sourceVectorHash:Hash|null}; sort recordedAtASC,eventIdASC, no rawbody/secret/token/recipient detail. Fullscope independent audit grant and per-row sourcefields required. No export endpoint in v1; owner58.02 controlled diagnostic package handles authorized sharing.

Service identity, exact schema/hash, tenant/environment/component scope ve original actor provenance birlikte kontrol edilir. Otomatik collector olgusu insan onayı üretmez. Human approval gerektiren portta background service original current mandate yerine geçemez. Her registered source row implementationState=NOT_VERIFIED, activation=DISABLED_UNTIL_OWNER_ACCEPTANCE_AND_FIXTURES; bu durum doküman yazılarak ENABLED olmaz.
